# Skills · K · part 46

842 entries, kv-cache-design to ky-sales-tax. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **kv-cache-design**: 优化 Agent 推理延迟与成本、诊断首 token 变慢或缓存命中率下降时使用——KV Cache 前缀不变性原则、三条铁律、五种破坏缓存的错误上下文管理模式、Chat Template 与历史思维链回传策略、缓存作为架构约束（缓存边界、子 Agent 字节级对齐、替换字符串冻结）。 ([bojieli/ai-agent-book](https://github.com/bojieli/ai-agent-book/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-cache-design) · ★59k)
- **kv-cache-enabled-generation-profiling**: Use when you need to measure the wall-clock time overhead that constrained generation (e.g., a prefix-tree index restricting valid token sequences) adds over normal LLM decoding under identical hardware and settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/kv-cache-enabled-generation-profiling))
- **kv-cache-eviction-eval**: Evaluates the quality of learned key-value (KV) cache eviction policies in preserving long-context reasoning and generation capabilities under strict memory constraints. It measures how well different compression strategies retain critical tokens without access to query-specific attention scores during the compression phase. Use when the user wants to benchmark on RULER-4k, OASST2-4k, BoolQ, ARC-Challenge, MMLU, HellaSwag, GovReport, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/kv-cache-eviction-eval) · ★3)
- **kv-cache-latency-budgeter**: Budget a specialist model fleet against the two production bottlenecks: KV-cache-bound concurrency and end-to-end latency. Computes how many concurrent users a GPU can host (peak KV per active user, not model size, is the binding constraint), proves that quantizing weights does not move that ceiling while KV compression (MEMENTO) does, estimates GPU speedups for graph analytics (cuGraph / nx-cugraph), and checks a multi-node workflow against the book's latency budget and the sub-2s target. Use when scaling a multi-model agent to real-time latency. NOT for model selection (that is model-routin… ([AnthonyAlcaraz/agentic-graph-rag-skills](https://github.com/AnthonyAlcaraz/agentic-graph-rag-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/optimization/kv-cache-latency-budgeter) · ★30)
- **kv-cache-offloading**: Extend LLM KV cache capacity by offloading to CPU RAM, disk, object storage or peer instances (vLLM OffloadingConnector, LMCache, SGLang HiCache, TensorRT-LLM host_cache_size, llama.cpp -cram, TabbyAPI), with exact per-engine flags. Use for "--kv-offloading-size", "--enable-hierarchical-cache", "host_cache_size", "-nkvo", low prefix cache hit rate, evicted prefixes, slow time to first token on long repeated prompts, or sharing KV across instances. ([ScaleDockHQ/llm-skills](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/llm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-cache-offloading))
- **kv-cache-oob-lane-architecture**: Architecture pattern for keeping local on-device LLMs fast and responsive by preserving KV-cache prefix stability (tail-bound volatile state) and serializing/yielding out-of-band (OOB) background tasks whenever the user sends a main chat message. ([ProjectMIKA/MIKA-Commons](https://github.com/ProjectMIKA/MIKA-Commons/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-cache-oob-lane-architecture) · ★2)
- **kv-cache-optimization**: KV-cache optimization patterns for LLM inference. Prefix caching, sliding window attention, cache reuse across turns, static cache for fixed prompts, and TTFT reduction strategies. Sources: huggingface/transformers (Apache-2.0). ([Horonomos-laps/Yana-AI](https://github.com/Horonomos-laps/Yana-AI/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kv-cache-optimization) · ★13 · 4 copies)
- **kv-cache-optimizer**: Specialized directive for prompt caching, token economy, RadixAttention tree-prefix caching, FlashAttention-3 chunked prefill, context compression (LLMLingua-2), SnapKV/PyramidKV eviction, and Cross-Model KV transfer. ([hammadshakeelai/prompt-engineering-mastery](https://github.com/hammadshakeelai/prompt-engineering-mastery/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/kv-cache-optimizer) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **kv-cache-prefix-optimizer**: Deterministic prompt prefix engineering & KV-cache hit optimization / Rekayasa prefix prompt deterministik & optimasi cache KV. ([roedyrustam/vibes-plug](https://github.com/roedyrustam/vibes-plug/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/kv-cache-prefix-optimizer) · ★128 · 2 copies)
- **kv-cache-quantization**: Fit more context or concurrent requests by quantizing the KV cache (FP8, NVFP4, int8, q8_0, q4_0) on vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp, Ollama, ExLlamaV3 and TensorFold, with exact per-engine flags and a quality gate. Use for "--kv-cache-dtype", "-ctk q8_0", "OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE", "cache_mode", KV cache OOM, out of KV blocks, "V cache quantization requires flash_attn", or long-context accuracy loss. ([ScaleDockHQ/llm-skills](https://github.com/ScaleDockHQ/llm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-cache-quantization))
- **kv-cache-recycling-to-expand-usable-context-capacity-in-low-parameter-llms**: **arXiv ID:** 2512.11851 **Authors:** Prashant Pandey **Published:** 2025-12-04T17:04:43Z **Abstract:** Whether attention key value (KV) states computed for one prompt for a small LLM can be reused to accelerate inference on a new similar prompt, giving an increase to the space to its context memory using an approach called token recycling. Using a standard Hugging Face setup with DialoGPT-medium ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/nlp-llm/kv-cache-recycling-to-expand-usable-context-capacity-in-low-parameter-llms) · ★4)
- **kv-cache-research**: Use this skill when the task is about Hopper experiment design, benchmark structure, ablations, or interpreting memory-versus-quality tradeoffs for packed-KV runtime work. ([OCWC22/Blackwell-and-Hopper-KV-Cache-Compression](https://github.com/OCWC22/Blackwell-and-Hopper-KV-Cache-Compression/tree/HEAD/Hopper/.agents/skills/kv-cache-research) · 2 copies)
- **kv-cache-research**: Use this skill when the task is about Blackwell experiment design, benchmark structure, ablations, or interpreting memory-versus-latency tradeoffs for the TRT-LLM + NVFP4 + offload tiered KV runtime. ([OCWC22/Blackwell-and-Hopper-KV-Cache-Compression](https://github.com/OCWC22/Blackwell-and-Hopper-KV-Cache-Compression/tree/HEAD/Blackwell/.agents/skills/kv-cache-research) · 2 copies)
- **kv-cache-steering-reasoning**: Guide frozen language models toward multi-step reasoning by modifying cached key-value representations after the prefilling stage. Extract steering vectors from contrastive prompt pairs and apply them to KV cache with scalar coefficients. Improves reasoning on GSM8K, ARC, CommonsenseQA while adding only 10ms overhead per token. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/kv-cache-steering-reasoning) · ★7)

- **kv-cinematic-intelligence-narrative**: 中文电影化悬疑智斗叙事与单配音解说引擎。Use when the user asks to create, rewrite, diagnose, or systematize 高悬疑、高反转、高智斗、高刺激、高情绪、高深度的故事文案，电影解说感、讲书人叙述、历史/新闻/案件智斗、双雄博弈、天人交战、心理悬疑、单人口播、听觉消歧，或将高考志愿、法律、AI、职场、商业、教育等知识赛道创新转译成可追看的电影化剧情。也用于选题筛选、经典叙事母本提炼、事实与戏剧边界管理和成品质量验收。 ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-cinematic-intelligence-narrative))
- **kv-clarity-mirror**: Runtime-focused AI metacognition layer for catching agent blind spots before delivery. Use this skill for medium/high-complexity or high-risk coding, research, debugging, planning, writing, quality reviews, hallucination prevention, self-checking, or when the user asks for critique, reliability, "别瞎编", "自我检查", "质量检查", "元认知", "盲区", "防幻觉", "有没有跑偏", "帮我挑刺", "review your own answer", "catch failure modes", "be critical". Also use it alongside domain skills when the task has many steps, unverifiable claims, tool use, or user assumptions that may be wrong. Do not trigger for trivial one-step tasks… ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-clarity-mirror))
- **kv-codex-troubleshoot**: OpenAI Codex Desktop (Windows) 故障诊断与修复决策树。当 Codex 出现"Windows 设置未完成"、无法发送消息、config 丢失、MCP 不加载、会话消失等问题时使用。 ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-codex-troubleshoot))
- **kv-cognitive-os**: 常驻认知操作系统。A层认知地板（自检/重构/分解/置信/完整）+ B层技能编排意识（41技能索引+路由+组合链）。所有Agent默认底座，无需显式调用即生效。 ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-cognitive-os))
- **kv-cong-van-giai-trinh-thue**: Tìm nguyên nhân chênh lệch cơ quan thuế nêu, soạn nháp công văn giải trình kèm bảng đối chiếu. Dùng khi nói "thuế yêu cầu giải trình", "viết công văn giải trình", "giải trình chênh lệch". ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/tree/HEAD/ke-toan/codex/.agents/skills/kv-cong-van-giai-trinh-thue) · ★22)
- **kv-creative-workbench**: Orchestrate an end-to-end creative decision workflow from a vague idea to a framed, narrowed, validated, and actionable direction. Use for complex ideation, product concepts, content or IP directions, business ideas, project selection, idea repair, or any request that needs two or more stages including reframing, divergence, convergence, opportunity validation, MVP design, or decision. Also use when the user mentions a creative workbench, a combined idea workflow, linked skills, or asks to turn a fuzzy thought into a testable plan. Chinese trigger phrases include 创意工作台, 商业创意, 鬼点子, 先发散再收敛, 从模糊… ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skill-suites/kv-creative-workbench/skills/kv-creative-workbench))
- **kv-dac-ta-yeu-cau-srs**: Lập tài liệu đặc tả yêu cầu phần mềm (SRS) khung: phạm vi, chức năng, phi chức năng, tích hợp, ma trận truy vết. - **kv-goal-loop**: Goal Mode / Loop Engineering 自主循环执行引擎。将高层目标转为自主循环： 计划→执行→验证→评估→迭代，直到目标达成或预算耗尽。 核心设计：执行者与评判者分离、停滞检测、上下文压缩、文件化状态。 当用户设定高层目标、要求"goal mode"、"自主循环"、"做到完成为止"、 "loop engineering"、"自动迭代"、"无人值守执行"、"持续优化直到"、 "跑个循环"、"自动执行直到完成"时触发。也适用于：复杂多步骤任务需要 自主分解并反复验证迭代的场景。不适用于：单步简单任务、一次性问答。 ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-goal-loop))
- **kv-goal-loop-chatgpt**: Run a bounded, same-turn plan-execute-verify-evaluate loop for complex goals in ChatGPT. Use when the user asks for goal mode, 自主循环、自动迭代、做到完成为止、持续优化、loop engineering, or gives a high-level multi-step objective that benefits from repeated execution and tool-based verification. The loop must complete within the current response/tool session, normally in no more than three rounds, and must never promise background or unattended continuation. Do not trigger for simple one-step tasks, pure questions, or work whose success cannot be meaningfully checked. ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-goal-loop-chatgpt))

- **kv-idea-convergence**: Independently review, narrow, transform, and prioritize a set of ideas without losing valuable creative cores. Use when the user asks to select, compare, rank, filter, converge, critique, reduce many ideas, rescue an impractical concept, or choose what to pursue. Also use when another skill sends CONVERGE, PRESERVE_CORE_CHANGE_FORM, REDUCE_SCOPE, or a candidate packet. This skill performs blind review and should not inherit the generator hype or rankings. Chinese trigger phrases include 创意收敛, 筛选点子, 审查创意, 提取创意核, 改造方案, and 从多个方向里选择. ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skill-suites/kv-creative-workbench/skills/kv-idea-convergence))
- **kv-insight-engine**: Positive capability amplifier from the KV skill line. Use this skill when the user explicitly wants stronger judgment, deeper framing, sharper synthesis, better prioritization, or a more senior-level recommendation — or when a task has multiple plausible paths and there is enough evidence to favor one. Good fits include coding, research, writing, product thinking, planning, strategy, critique, and redesign when the user asks for "更深一点", "更强一点", "更高级", "别只做表面", "帮我提纯", "提高判断", "给更好的方案", "像高手一样想", "look deeper", or "give me the stronger version". ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/tree/HEAD/hoc-thuat-nghien-cuu/codex/.agents/skills/kv-ke-hoach-phan-tich-du-lieu) · ★22)
- **kv-ke-hoach-san-xuat-tuan-theo-may**: Lập kế hoạch sản xuất tuần theo năng lực từng máy/chuyền: xếp đơn hàng, tính giờ máy, chỉ ra máy quá tải, đơn trễ hạn. Dùng khi nói "kế hoạch sản xuất tuần", "xếp máy", "cân tải". - **kv-ke-hoach-tai-chinh-ca-nhan-khach**: Lập kế hoạch tài chính cá nhân cho khách: dòng tiền tháng, quỹ dự phòng, mục tiêu tiết kiệm, sức vay - không khuyến nghị đầu tư cụ thể. Dùng khi nói "tư vấn tài chính cá nhân", "lập ngân sách". ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/tree/HEAD/tai-chinh-ngan-hang/codex/.agents/skills/kv-ke-hoach-tai-chinh-ca-nhan-khach) · ★22)

Dùng khi nói "kế hoạch truyền thông", "đề cương chiến dịch", "IMC plan". - **kv-ke-hoach-truyen-thong-su-kien**: Lập kế hoạch truyền thông cho sự kiện, ra mắt, họp báo: mục tiêu đo được, thông điệp, kênh, lịch trước-trong-sau, ngân sách, phân công. Dùng khi nói "kế hoạch truyền thông", "media plan sự kiện". - **kv-ke-hoach-tuyen-dai-ly**: Lập kế hoạch tuyển dụng đại lý bảo hiểm cho trưởng nhóm: chân dung ứng viên, kênh tìm, tin tuyển dụng trung thực, buổi giới thiệu nghề, lộ trình 90 ngày. Dùng khi nói "tuyển đại lý", "mở rộng nhóm". ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/tree/HEAD/bao-hiem/codex/.agents/skills/kv-ke-hoach-tuyen-dai-ly) · ★22)
- **kv-khai-bao-dung-ai-hoc-thuat**: Rà việc dùng AI trong luận văn, bài báo theo quy định của trường / tạp chí, ghi nhật ký và soạn đoạn khai báo minh bạch. Dùng khi nói "khai báo dùng AI", "trường có cho dùng ChatGPT không". - **kv-khao-sat-gan-ket-nhan-vien**: Thiết kế khảo sát gắn kết nhân viên ẩn danh và phân tích kết quả: điểm theo nhóm, ý kiến mở, việc nên làm. Dùng khi nói "khảo sát hài lòng", "phân tích phiếu khảo sát", "chỉ số hài lòng". Dùng khi nói "kịch bản telesales", "gọi điện cho khách", "cold call". - **kv-language-ideation**: Reframe restrictive language and generate genuinely different creative directions before feasibility analysis. Use for brainstorming, concept creation, naming systems, content or product ideation, research-question reframing, non-obvious angles, creative blocks, or requests to open the problem space. Also use when another skill sends FRAME, DIVERGE, REFRAME_ROOT, EXPAND_DIVERSITY, or REFRAME_TARGET_USER. Do not use as the primary skill when the user only needs commercial validation or final prioritization. Chinese trigger phrases include 创意发散, 语言框架, 重构问题, 打开脑洞, 重新命名, and 语言造境. ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skill-suites/kv-creative-workbench/skills/kv-language-ideation))
- **kv-lap-bep-ke-hoach-thuc-hien-bim**: Soạn kế hoạch thực hiện BIM (BEP) đáp ứng từng mục EIR: tổ chức, phần mềm, mức chi tiết, phối hợp, kiểm soát chất lượng, mốc bàn giao. Dùng khi nói "viết BEP", "kế hoạch BIM", "trả lời EIR". ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/tree/HEAD/xay-dung/codex/.agents/skills/kv-lap-bep-ke-hoach-thuc-hien-bim) · ★22)

- **kv-sanity-auditor**: [kv-] 苛刻产品体验官 v2 — 六重视角的融合审查官：严苛产品经理 + 高级 UX 设计师 + 资深前端/全栈 + QA 验收工程师 + 开源生态顾问 + 挑剔真实用户。 不只是"挑刺"，而是"补齐常识"——AI 开发时最容易遗漏的那些"本来就该有"的功能。 核心能力： ├─ 默认预期推断引擎：识别产品类型 → 自动推断"这种产品应该有什么" ├─ 10 个通用体验维度 × 领域专项（游戏/工具/Dashboard/内容站/后台/AI工具） ├─ 开源方案知识库 + 外部生态联动（Context7 / shadcn / MCP Registry） ├─ 输出即可执行：每个问题附带开发任务（含文件/状态/验收标准） ├─ 用户路径模拟：新手/熟手/恶劣环境/异常路径 └─ 可进化记忆：每次审计后生成 Memory Patch，沉淀可复用常识 两个典型用法： ① 审查模式：给一个已建成的产品 URL/截图/描述，深度审查并输出改进清单 + 开发任务 ② 前置注入：开发新功能前，先跑一遍这个 skill，把该有的东西列出来再动手 ([Kvxw1105/kv-skills](https://github.com/Kvxw1105/kv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kv-sanity-auditor))
- **kv-session-management**: Implement session storage and caching on Cloudflare Workers using KV. Applies to TypeScript Workers with @cloudflare/workers-types. ([eykd/vance-ts](https://github.com/eykd/vance-ts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kv-session-management))

- **kv-store-grpc**: Guide for implementing gRPC-based key-value store services in Python. This skill should be used when building gRPC servers with protobuf definitions, implementing KV store operations (Get, Set, Delete), or troubleshooting gRPC service connectivity. Applicable to tasks involving grpcio, protobuf code generation, and background server processes. ([Zurybr/lefarma-skills](https://github.com/Zurybr/lefarma-skills/tree/HEAD/letta/benchmarks/trajectory-feedback/kv-store-grpc) · ★1)
- **kv-store-grpc**: Guidance for building gRPC-based key-value store services in Python. This skill should be used when tasks involve creating gRPC servers, defining protocol buffer schemas, or implementing key-value storage APIs with gRPC. Covers proto file creation, code generation, server implementation, and verification strategies. ([Zurybr/lefarma-skills](https://github.com/Zurybr/lefarma-skills/tree/HEAD/letta/benchmarks/trajectory-only/kv-store-grpc) · ★1)
- **kv-streams-agentic-context-compaction**: KV-streams methodology — streaming the KV cache forward across compaction events instead of flushing it, for trainable constant-memory long-context agentic RL. Use when: (1) context compaction forces re-prefill and kills training throughput, (2) compaction loses long-range info the policy still needs, (3) agent RL traces overflow GPU memory. Keywords: KV cache streaming, context compaction, agentic RL, recurrent state, training throughput. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/multi-agent-rl/kv-streams-agentic-context-compaction) · ★4)
- **kv-system-check**: Read-only inventory and go/no-go for the kv-cache stack — software versions, GPU/RDMA/KV hardware, co-tenancy, container state and plugin parity across all three nodes. Mutates nothing. Also trigger with /kv-system-check. ([pmallapp-amd/llm-stacks](https://github.com/pmallapp-amd/llm-stacks/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kv-system-check) · ★1)
- **kv-tai-lieu-giao-duc-suc-khoe**: Viết tờ rơi, hướng dẫn chăm sóc tại nhà cho người bệnh bằng lời dễ hiểu, chỉ dựa trên tài liệu chuyên môn bạn đưa. - **kv-tier-management**: Manage runtime KV cache tiers (GPU → CPU RAM → local disk) for LLM serving: decide when KV is stored, retained, prefetched, or evicted; size the concurrency-versus-RAM-pressure trade-off with an explicit in-flight admission dial; order RAM-release levers; and measure with token-level L1/L2 hit rates and prefetch-pressure counters. Use when configuring KV offload for a serving stack. Covers runtime KV offload only — model startup/loading, checkpoint save/restore, and block-device profiling are separate concerns (see weight-loading-optimization).
